Experiments on HumanEval Plus, MBPP, MATH-500, and ASQA, spanning agentic code generation, mathematical reasoning, and retrieval-augmented long-form question answering, demonstrate that ProgRouter reduces the operating cost relative to key baselines while maintaining strong task-solving performance.

</details>


### [210] [AsymSpec: Context-Asymmetric Speculative Decoding for Agentic LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26004)
*Sheng Liang,Yongyue Zhang,Nathanael Brian,Hang Lv,Hao Wang,Chen Zhang,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AsymSpec 通过让小型草稿模型读取完整上下文、让大型验证模型读取压缩上下文，并用对比式 δ-fusion 与分歧感知接受门控协同生成，在显著降低推理成本的同时尽量保持完整上下文精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic LLM 管线在检索、工具调用和多轮交互中会积累大量上下文，导致推理成本和延迟上升。常规输入压缩能降本但损害准确率；传统 speculative decoding 虽可无损加速生成，但要求草稿模型与验证模型使用相同上下文，无法解决“压缩降本 vs 精度损失”的矛盾。

Method: 提出 AsymSpec 非对称 speculative decoding 框架：轻量 drafter 使用完整输入上下文，大型 verifier 使用压缩后的上下文；drafter 通过对比式 δ-fusion 调整/引导 verifier 的 logits；同时引入 divergence-aware acceptance gate，根据两者分歧调节接受机制，以维持验证稳定性和较高草稿接受率。

Result: 在四类 agentic 能力与两个端到端 agent benchmark 上评估，AsymSpec 平均达到约 90% 的完整上下文准确率；在独立文本能力任务上实现 1.3–1.7 倍吞吐加速，并仅消耗约 0.2–0.3 倍计算成本。

Conclusion: 非对称上下文访问可以在压缩丢失关键推理信号的场景中显著改善精度—成本权衡，为低延迟、低成本的 agentic LLM 推理提供有效方向。

Abstract: Agentic LLM pipelines face escalating inference costs as context accumulates across retrieval, tool use, and multi-turn interactions. To control latency, deployments routinely compress inputs, but this degrades task accuracy. Speculative decoding (SD) accelerates generation losslessly, yet it assumes the drafter and verifier share an identical context, preventing SD from resolving the accuracy-overhead trade-off. We propose AsymSpec, an asymmetric speculative decoding framework that breaks this symmetry: a lightweight drafter reads the full input while the large verifier operates on the compressed view. The drafter steers the verifier via a contrastive $δ$-fusion of logits, modulated by a divergence-aware acceptance gate that preserves verification stability and high draft acceptance rates. Evaluated across four agentic capabilities and two end-to-end agent benchmarks, AsymSpec reaches $\approx 90\%$ of full-context accuracy on average, delivering $1.3$--$1.7\times$ throughput speedups at $0.2$--$0.3\times$ the compute cost on isolated text capabilities. These results show that asymmetric context access yields substantial gains precisely when compression discards critical reasoning signals.

</details>


### [211] [Imitation Learning for Connection-Tableau Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26009)
*Fredrik Rømming,Mantas Bakšys,Martin S. Fixman,Sean B. Holden*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文将自动定理证明中的证明构造统一建模为形式演算诱导的转移系统中的策略学习问题，并用图神经网络学习证明编辑选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自动定理证明器依赖大量符号搜索脚手架与启发式，在不同搜索范式之间接口不统一，且难以充分利用跨问题可迁移的结构信息。论文希望用统一的状态化策略框架描述 leanCoP、plCoP/rlCoP 等证明搜索/规划过程，并让学习方法直接介入决策。

Method: 作者把 clausal connection tableaux 下的证明构造表示为转移系统中的状态与动作，形式演算限定合法且可靠的证明步骤；将 leanCoP 式搜索和 plCoP/rlCoP 式规划统一为同一接口上的状态策略。进一步使用图神经网络对证明编辑动作进行打分，从已找到的证明中通过模仿学习训练策略，并在逐步移除符号回溯等搜索脚手架的设置下评估性能。

Result: 在 M2k、MPTP2078-bushy 和 TPTP v9.2.1 数据集上，在固定步数预算内，学习到的策略相比 leanCoP 最多多解决 46% 的问题，并且通常能以少一个数量级的步骤找到证明。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将自动定理证明建模为统一的策略学习问题，并结合可迁移结构表示的图神经网络，可以显著提升连接表演算证明搜索效率；即使减少传统符号搜索支撑，学习策略仍能保持较强性能。

Abstract: An automated theorem prover builds a proof step by step, choosing at each point what to add and what to remove. We cast this construction as a policy acting in a transition system induced by a formal calculus, which fixes which steps are sound: for clausal connection tableaux, leanCoP-style search and plCoP/rlCoP-style planning then become stateful policies over one interface, and policy-learning methods apply directly. We equip such policies with a graph neural network that scores proof edits from structure that transfers across problems, train it by imitation learning from found proofs, and measure how performance holds as we remove search scaffolding, from full symbolic backtracking to a policy the network drives alone. Within a fixed step budget on M2k, MPTP2078-bushy, and TPTP v9.2.1, learned policies solve up to 46% more problems than leanCoP, and reach proofs in an order of magnitude fewer steps.

</details>


### [212] [Trace Integrity for LLM Data Agents: A Vision for Auditable Structured Reasoning in Real-World Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26036)
*Srimonti Dutta,Akshata Kishore Moharir*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出仅用答案准确率评估 LLM 数据代理可靠性不足，提出 Trace Integrity 作为可部署可靠性标准，并用 CAIT Rate 衡量“答案正确但计算轨迹无效”的风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在结构化数据任务中，模型可能给出基准判定正确的答案，但其背后的推理或查询轨迹并不合法、不可执行或不可审计；自然语言推理与自由形式解释无法可靠表达真实系统所需的算子级程序，因此需要新的可靠性评估信号。

Method: 论文定义 Trace Integrity，要求答案背后的计算记录具备显式性、可执行性、模式合法性、算子忠实性、可重放性、答案一致性和可审计性；提出执行契约，将用户意图、模式元素、算子计划、假设、可执行查询、验证状态和最终答案关联起来；同时提出 CAIT Rate 衡量答案正确但轨迹无效的比例。

Result: 在 BIRD Mini-Dev 上，Direct SQL、Operation Summary + SQL、Contract-First SQL 的答案准确率分别为 20%、22%、24%；Trace Integrity 通过率分别为 39%、43%、40%；CAIT Rate 分别为 55%、59.1%、45.8%，说明答案准确率、轨迹有效性和静默失败风险是不同信号。

Conclusion: 真实部署中的 LLM 数据代理不能只按最终答案是否匹配参考答案来评估，还应检查答案是否由可执行、可验证、可审计的计算过程支撑；Trace Integrity 与 CAIT Rate 可补充传统答案准确率评估。

Abstract: Answer accuracy is an insufficient reliability signal for LLM data agents. In structured-data tasks, a benchmark-correct answer can be produced by an invalid trace. This paper introduces Trace Integrity, a deployment reliability criterion for evaluating whether the computation recorded behind an answer is explicit, executable, schema-valid, operator-faithful, replayable, answer-consistent, and auditable. We identify the Structure Gap as the deployment failure mode that makes Trace Integrity necessary: natural-language reasoning and free-form rationales do not reliably specify the operator-level programs required by real-world systems. We operationalize Trace Integrity with execution contracts, structured artifacts that bind user intent to schema elements, operator plans, assumptions, executable queries, verification status, and final-answer linkage. We also introduce CAIT (Correct Answer / Invalid Trace) Rate, which measures how often answer-only evaluation counts computationally unsupported outputs as successes. In an empirical demonstration on BIRD Mini-Dev, Direct SQL, Operation Summary + SQL, and Contract-First SQL achieve answer accuracies of 20%, 22%, and 24%, while their Trace Integrity Pass Rates are 39%, 43%, and 40% and their CAIT Rates remain high at 55%, 59.1%, and 45.8%, showing that answer accuracy, trace validity, and silent-failure risk are distinct evaluation signals. Real-world LLM data agents should, therefore, be evaluated not only by whether their outputs match a reference answer, but by whether those outputs are backed by auditable computation.

</details>


### [213] [SwarmWorld: Stigmergic technological evolution in societies of language-model agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26081)
*Subhadeep Pal,Fiona Y. Wang,Markus J. Buehler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该摘要提出 SwarmWorld：一组初始同质的 LLM 智能体在共享空间环境中，无需预设角色、配方或中心化流程，通过物理痕迹与协作逐步自组织为具备技术积累能力的社会。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体系统多依赖直接对话、固定角色或中心化工作流，尚不清楚去中心化 LLM 智能体能否仅通过共享环境产生功能性技术，并相较独立搜索获得优势。

Method: 构建 SwarmWorld 环境，让智能体探索空间、处理资源、测试材料、建造持久人工物，并编写可执行控制器；控制器由确定性模拟器在智能体移除后、面对未见扰动时评估。系统将智能体的认知提案与世界中的实际功能后果分离，并与强 best-of-N 独立搜索基线比较。

Result: 共享社会形成了更广、更鲁棒的技术组合，但在最强单个人工物上，独立搜索仍具竞争力。智能体自发分化出探索、建造、维护与协调等行为，并随世界成熟发生角色转变。多数复用源于对物理人工物的观察，而非直接通信。

Conclusion: 物理式间接协作足以支持具备能力的技术社会；交互更主要促进持续的技术生态与组织化积累，而不必然带来单个发明的全面优势。显式文化机制可增强协作与组织，但其功能收益取决于任务结果与时间尺度。

Abstract: Collective intelligence can emerge when individuals coordinate through a shared environment, allowing local actions to accumulate into durable social organization. Language-model agents offer a new substrate for this process, yet most multi-agent systems rely on direct conversation, predefined roles, or centralized workflows. It remains unclear whether decentralized agents can build functional technologies and outperform independent search. Here, initially homogeneous LLM agents in SwarmWorld self-organize without assigned roles or recipes into evolving technological societies. Agents explore a spatial environment, process resources, test materials, construct persistent artifacts, and write executable controllers evaluated by a deterministic simulator under unseen disturbances after the agents are removed. SwarmWorld splits cognition from consequence: agents propose architectures and controllers within fixed action and material schemas, while the simulated world determines function. Shared societies develop broader, more resilient technological portfolios than a strong best-of-N isolated-search baseline, although isolated search remains competitive for the strongest artifact. Agents differentiate into exploration, construction, maintenance, and coordination behaviors, transitioning as the world matures. Technologies accumulate through collaborative construction, executable inheritance, and persistent agent-artifact networks, with most reuse beginning through physical observation rather than communication. Explicit cultural mechanisms amplify collaboration and organization, but functional benefits depend on outcome and timescale. Physical stigmergy alone supports capable societies, while interaction drives persistent technological ecologies rather than universally superior individual inventions.

</details>


### [214] [Planetary Prediction Engine: Autonomous Geospatial Prediction via Intelligent Data Selection and Foundation Model Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26088)
*Evelyn Ma,Rama Kumar Pasumarthi,Kishwar Shafin,Mandar Sharma,Mimi Sun,Hamed Sadeghi,Dav M. Ebengo,Mbulayi Onesime,Rouslan Solomakhin,John Wamburu,William Ogallo,Aisha Walcott-Bryant,Sanxing Chen,Arbaaz Muslim,Yael Mayer,Ronald Ho,Roy Lee,Ruth Alcantara,Abdoulaye Diack,Monica Bharel,Lambert Rosique,Jeremy Amez-Droz,Christopher Haire,James Manyika,Yossi Matias,Niv Efron,Gautam Prasad,Shravya Shetty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Planetary Prediction Engine（PPE），一个可从自然语言查询自动完成地理空间数据检索、融合、模型搜索与预测的端到端 AI 系统，并在健康、风险、脆弱性、粮食安全和流行病预测等任务上优于专家基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真地理空间建模对粮食安全、灾害风险、疾病暴发和社会经济脆弱性等全球挑战至关重要，但现有流程受限于数据生态碎片化、人工检索、多模态数据清洗融合和反复模型选择，导致部署门槛高、效率低。

Method: PPE 从自然语言问题出发，自动在开放网络与地球观测平台（如 Data Commons、Google Earth Engine）中检索时空相关协变量，并与地理空间基础模型嵌入（PDFM、AlphaEarth）融合；同时针对任务搜索合适模型架构族，并加入自动过拟合防护机制。

Result: 在多个领域和地理区域任务中，PPE 超过 SOTA 或人工调参专家基线：美国 21 个 CDC 健康指标平均 R² 为 76.8%（基线 60.0%），FEMA 风险指数为 64.9%（60.0%），社会脆弱性指数为 66.2%（58.6%）；尼日利亚粮食安全指标下采样/降尺度任务 R² 为 66.1%（31.5%）；2026 年 DRC Bundibugyo Ebola nowcasting 的 Recall@10 为 83.3%，较公开 SOTA 高 10.3 个百分点。

Conclusion: PPE 通过自动化多模态行星尺度数据发现与面向任务的模型优化，显著降低地理空间预测建模的技术门槛，可支持快速、定制化、专家级的行星尺度分析与部署。

Abstract: Addressing critical global challenges, from food security and disaster risk to disease outbreaks and socio-economic vulnerability, demands high-fidelity geospatial modeling. However, building predictive planetary models remains bottlenecked by a fragmented data ecosystem, requiring manual data retrieval, multimodal data curation and fusion along with iterative model selection. We present the Planetary Prediction Engine (PPE), an autonomous AI system that executes this end-to-end workflow directly from natural-language queries. PPE synthesizes multimodal datasets on the fly, retrieving spatiotemporally relevant covariates across open-web and Earth observation platforms (Data Commons, Google Earth Engine) and fusing them with geospatial foundation model embeddings (PDFM, AlphaEarth). Simultaneously, it searches over task-tailored model architecture families with automated overfitting guards. Across diverse tasks, geographies, and scientific domains, PPE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art or manually tuned expert baselines. For US spatial regression, PPE improves mean $R^2$ across 21 CDC health indicators (76.8% vs. 60.0%), FEMA national risk indices (64.9% vs. 60.0%), and the Social Vulnerability Index (66.2% vs. 58.6%). For spatial downscaling in data-scarce settings, PPE integrates localized proxies to double baseline accuracy in Nigerian food security indicators ($R^2$ of 66.1% vs. 31.5%). For epidemiological nowcasting of the 2026 DRC Bundibugyo Ebola outbreak, PPE achieves a Recall@10 of 83.3% (identifying 15 of 18 newly invaded health zones across five weekly forecasts), a +10.3 percentage-point improvement over the public state-of-the-art modeling (~73%). By combining autonomous multimodal planetary data discovery with targeted model optimization, PPE lowers the technical barrier to planetary-scale analytics, enabling rapid, customized, expert-level deployment.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [215] [Towards Faithful and Efficient Semantic Communication: An Ontological Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25422)
*Yixiao Feng,Yueting Wang,Yining Wang,Han Han,Bo Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文提出面向多视角视觉问答任务的本体驱动语义通信框架，通过共享本体知识库压缩、对齐并校验场景图语义信息，在显著降低传输数据量的同时提升问答准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多发射端使用视觉语言模型从不同视角提取场景图并传输给接收端，但由于模型输出存在冗余、歧义和跨视角不一致等问题，直接传输完整场景图效率低且可能影响多视角VQA性能。

Method: 构建发射端与接收端共享的本体知识库，预定义同义词、推理规则和一致性约束。发射端利用推理规则删除可由知识库推断出的部分场景图以减少传输；接收端利用同义词进行跨视角语义对齐，并依据约束检测多视角语义不一致。同时定义多视角VQA准确率指标MVA用于评估。

Result: 仿真结果表明，与传输完整场景图相比，该框架最多可减少87.1%的语义信息数据量，并提升4.5%的回答准确率；与语义信息过滤方法相比，MVA最多提升16.0%。

Conclusion: 本体驱动的语义通信能够有效缓解VLM提取场景图中的冗余、歧义和不一致问题，在多视角VQA中实现通信开销降低与任务性能提升的双重收益。

Abstract: In this paper, an ontology-driven semantic communication (ODSC) framework is proposed for multi-view visual question answering (VQA) tasks. In the considered framework, multiple transmitters observe a scene, extract the semantic information (SI) with vision-language models (VLMs), and transmit the scene graphs to a receiver. Due to the completeness, heterogeneity, and uninterpretability of the VLMs, the extracted scene graphs are redundant, ambiguous, and inconsistent. To solve these problems, the transmitters and the receiver share an ontology-based knowledge base that predefines synonyms, inference rules, and consistency constraints. For each transmitter, the proposed ODSC framework removes the partial scene graph that can be inferred based on the inference rules. For the receiver, the proposed framework aligns the SI of different views based on the synonyms and detects the inconsistency among the views based on the constraints. A metric of multi-view VQA accuracy (MVA) is defined to evaluate the proposed framework. Simulation results show that, compared with transmitting the complete scene graphs, the proposed framework reduces the data size of the SI by up to 87.1% while improving the answering accuracy by 4.5%. Moreover, the proposed framework yields up to a 16.0% improvement in terms of the MVA compared with the SI filtering approaches.

</details>


### [216] [Conditional Total Correlation and the Serial Depth of Adaptive Parallel Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25505)
*Chuling Wen,Weijie Liang,Jian Lu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究离散向量的自适应并行采样，证明任意揭示策略的前向 KL 散度等于各轮累积的期望条件总相关，从而把“轮内并行”的误差精确刻画为条件依赖的信息成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受掩码扩散模型并行解码启发，作者希望理解在按轮并行采样离散变量时，哪些目标分布或揭示策略能以较少串行轮数达到给定近似误差，并解释并行化能力的本质来源。

Method: 设定一种自适应并行采样框架：每轮策略根据已观测值确定要揭示的未观测坐标，并从精确条件边缘分布独立采样。以 forward KL 衡量近似误差，以达到误差预算所需的目标平均轮数定义串行深度。核心理论工具是将策略散度分解为每轮的条件总相关，并据此分析多类结构化分布与揭示顺序。

Result: 证明了散度等于期望条件总相关累积的精确恒等式；得到有限阶马尔可夫链的零误差调度，其轮复杂度与马尔可夫阶成正比、与长度对数相关；刻画 Bernoulli walk 的对数深度；展示从左到右与层次揭示之间线性对数分离；证明均匀随机排列在固定预算下需线性期望轮数；给出若干组合分布的深度阶，包括平衡二进制串的平方对数深度、一热块的平方根深度及矩形变体的多项式指数族。实验上，伪成本能区分实际解码规则，且策略排序与自采样输出质量高度一致。

Conclusion: 论文表明，串行深度不能由熵或负对数似然简单决定；目标分布的条件依赖结构才是并行采样/解码可并行化程度的关键决定因素。

Abstract: Motivated by parallel decoding in masked diffusion models, we study adaptive parallel sampling of discrete vectors: in each round, a deterministic policy selects unrevealed coordinates on the basis of the values observed so far, and the selected coordinates are sampled independently from their exact conditional marginals. Approximation error is measured by forward Kullback-Leibler divergence, and serial depth is the minimum target-averaged number of rounds meeting a prescribed error budget. Our central result is an exact identity: the divergence of every policy equals the expected conditional total correlation accumulated over its reveal rounds, so conditional total correlation is the exact information cost of within-round parallelism. The identity yields zero-error schedules for finite-order Markov chains with round complexity proportional to the Markov order and logarithmic in sequence length, a matching logarithmic characterization of the Bernoulli walk at every fixed error budget, and a linear-versus-logarithmic separation between left-to-right and hierarchical reveal orders. Uniform random permutations require linearly many expected rounds at every fixed budget; their hard-cap round-error tradeoff is an exact integer-composition problem whose fixed-round asymptotics and joint-scaling frontier we determine. Uniform balanced binary strings have depth of order squared logarithm, and binary one-hot blocks have square-root depth, with rectangular versions realizing every polynomial exponent up to one half. These results separate serial depth from entropy and negative log-likelihood, and establish conditional-dependence structure as a fundamental determinant of parallelizability. Experiments with a masked diffusion language model show that the pseudo-cost distinguishes deployed decoding rules and that its policy rankings agree closely with the quality of self-sampled outputs.

</details>


### [217] [Joint Beamforming Design and Port Selection in Fluid Antenna-Assisted Multi-Cell Networks: A Personalized Federated Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25514)
*Liwen Gao,Li Zheng,Xing Hao,Ziru Chen,Lin X. Cai*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文研究多小区流体天线辅助网络中的联合波束成形与端口选择，提出基于联邦表示学习的PA-DNN，在保护本地差异性的同时提升加权和速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流体天线系统中，连续的主动波束成形与离散的天线端口选择相互耦合，并受小区内/小区间干扰影响；传统集中式或常规联邦学习方法难以高效适应异构小区环境。

Method: 构建FedRep框架与位置感知双分支深度神经网络。模型以信道状态信息和端口位置编码为输入，分别通过任务分支输出波束成形向量和端口选择；联邦训练中共享与波束成形相关的全局表示参数，同时保留端口选择相关参数在本地更新。

Result: 仿真表明，该方法相比常规联邦学习和端口选择基准方案可获得更高的加权和速率。

Conclusion: 通过将位置感知神经网络与联邦表示学习结合，论文实现了对多小区FAS网络中联合优化问题的分布式、高适应性求解，并提升系统频谱效率。

Abstract: This paper investigates joint beamforming and port selection in multi-cell fluid antenna-assisted (FAS) networks. In such networks, active beamforming and discrete FA port selection are coupled through intra-cell and inter-cell interference and are jointly optimized to maximize the weighted sum-rate (WSR). We develop a federated representation learning (FedRep) framework with a position-aware dual-branch deep neural network (PA-DNN). The PA-DNN uses channel state information and port positional encoding as inputs, and jointly outputs beamforming vectors and port selections through two task-specific branches. To support decentralized training across heterogeneous cells, the FedRep framework shares global beamforming-related parameters among base stations while keeping port-selection parameters local for cell-specific adaptation. Simulation results show that the proposed scheme achieves a higher weighted sum-rate than conventional FL and port-selection benchmark schemes.

</details>


### [218] [Constrained Maximum Entropy Contiguous Aggregations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25533)
*Roberto Bruno,Ugo Vaccaro*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究在熵上限约束下，将给定概率分布按连续区间聚合为指定规模分布，并最大化聚合后香农熵的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续聚合在需要保持原始顺序的概率压缩、分箱、数据摘要等场景中出现；作者关注如何在信息量受约束时获得尽可能高熵、即更均衡的信息表示。

Method: 形式化定义连续聚合与熵约束优化问题；提出一个可精确求解的动态规划算法，并设计两个更高效的贪心算法以获得接近最优的解。

Result: 动态规划算法能够精确求解该问题；两个贪心算法在时间效率上更优，并能提供接近最优的聚合结果。

Conclusion: 该工作为带香农熵约束的有序概率分布聚合提供了精确算法和实用近似算法，适用于多种需要连续分组与信息控制的应用场景。

Abstract: Given a probability distribution $p = (p_1, \dots, p_n)$ and an integer $1\leq m \leq n$, a contiguous aggregation of $p$ is a probability distribution $q = (q_1, \dots, q_m)$ such that each $q_i$ is a sum of consecutive elements of $p$. Given $p$ and a positive number $R$, we consider the problem of computing a maximum entropy contiguous aggregation $q$ of $p$, under the constraint that its Shannon entropy $H(q)$ is at most $R$. We devise a dynamic programming algorithm that solves the problem exactly, and two time-efficient greedy algorithms that provide close-to-optimal solutions. We discuss a few scenarios where our problem arises.

</details>


### [219] [Quantitative tiling stability from quadratic discrepancy in Hamming spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25716)
*Valery,Grishin,Aryeh Lev Zabokritskiy*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究有限汉明空间中二次球差异能量的稳定性：当参数对应完美码时，超过完美码基准的差异量可定量控制球覆盖重数偏离1的程度，并推广到若干一纠错、二纠错及经典完美码族情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有结果表明在完美码参数下，二次球差异的精确极小化子是完美码。本文希望进一步回答稳定性问题：若一个码的差异能量接近完美码最优值，它在球覆盖意义上是否也接近完美铺砌；同时希望该结论不依赖线性、最小距离或纠错约束。

Method: 固定字母表大小、长度和码大小，在所有同参数码之间比较二次球差异。作者建立“铺砌缺陷稳定性”不等式，将超额差异与特殊球覆盖重数相对1的平方偏差联系起来；针对一纠错参数利用球装箱与Lloyd整性条件给出下界系数，对二纠错参数在Lloyd根整性条件下给出显式正系数，并结合重复码、Golay码族的直接证书与完美码分类。

Result: 一纠错参数下得到下界系数κ_{n,q}/q^2≥1，且统一下界1是尖锐的，但参数相关的认证系数可显著更大；同时给出显式参数相关上界估计。二纠错参数在n≥5、球装箱整除性和两个不同整数Lloyd根条件下，无需假设完美码存在即可得到显式正系数；q≥4时有闭式表达和固定字母表渐近式。结合经典码族可覆盖所有非平凡完美码。

Conclusion: 二次球差异不仅刻画完美码的最优性，还具有强稳定性：能量超额控制球覆盖缺陷、洞、重叠、异常点，以及均匀球噪声输出相对均匀分布的总变差和Rényi散度等统计偏离。该稳定性关注覆盖重数分布，而非与某个特定完美码的对称差距离。

Abstract: Quadratic ball discrepancy defines an energy on codes in finite Hamming spaces. At perfect-code parameters, its exact minimizers are the perfect codes. We fix the alphabet size, length, and code cardinality and compare all codes with these parameters. We prove tiling-defect stability: excess discrepancy above the perfect-code benchmark controls the squared deviation of the distinguished ball-covering multiplicity from one. For one-error parameters satisfying sphere-packing and Lloyd integrality, the lower coefficient is $κ_{n,q}/q^2\geq1$. The uniform floor one is sharp, while the certified parameter-dependent coefficient can be much larger. An explicit parameter-dependent upper estimate is also available, and the two certified coefficients can be far apart. For any two-error parameter pair with $n\geq5$ satisfying sphere-packing divisibility and having two distinct integral Lloyd roots in the Hamming weight range, we obtain an explicit positive coefficient without assuming that a perfect code exists. For alphabets of size at least four, this conditional coefficient has a closed form and fixed-alphabet asymptotics. Direct certificates for the repetition and Golay families, combined with perfect-code classification, give tiling-defect stability for every nontrivial perfect code. Here stability concerns the ball-covering multiplicity profile, not symmetric-difference proximity to a particular perfect code. The defect is also a normalized chi-square smoothing error under uniform ball noise, so excess discrepancy controls holes, overlaps, defective ambient points, total variation, and Rényi divergence from uniformity of the ball-noise output. Competing codes need not be linear or satisfy a distance or error-correction constraint.

</details>


### [220] [Beyond Minimum Distance: The Optimal Leading Coefficient in the High-SNR Error-Probability Expansion for AWGN Spherical Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25805)
*Nikola Zlatanov*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究高信噪比下球面码的最大似然误码概率精确渐近项，指出随信噪比重新优化码本可比固定 packing-optimal 码本获得更小的领先系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 packing-optimal 球面码最大化最小距离，从而在高信噪比下达到最优误差指数；但在误差指数相同的情况下，精确误码概率的领先系数仍可能不同。作者关注是否通过针对每个信噪比重新优化码本，进一步降低高信噪比下的精确误码率。

Method: 论文建立固定 packing-optimal 码本与 SNR-wise 优化码本的最大似然误码概率渐近展开，比较其领先系数；证明 K*_{M,n}≤K^{fix}_{M,n}，并用 Gaussian soft-packing energy 的极限刻画 K*_{M,n}。此外，通过 orthoplex-bound 构造解释严格不等式的产生机制。

Result: 对于 2≤M≤n+1，证明 K*_{M,n}=K^{fix}_{M,n}=M(M−1)。对于 M=n+k 且 2≤k≤n，证明 K^{fix}_{n+k,n}=4n(k−1)，且 K*_{n+k,n}≤4k(k−1)。特别地，当 k=2 且 n≥3 时，K*_{n+2,n}=8<4n=K^{fix}_{n+2,n}，误差渐近项改进因子为 n/2；当 k=n 时两者均为 4n(n−1)。

Conclusion: SNR-wise 码本优化在某些参数区间可在不牺牲最优误差指数的前提下降低精确误码概率的领先系数，说明高信噪比性能不仅由最小距离决定，也受最近点对结构及其可扰动性影响。

Abstract: Packing-optimal $(M,n)$ spherical codes attain the largest achievable minimum distance and hence the optimal error exponent at high SNR. Among these codebooks held fixed as SNR grows, the smallest leading coefficient, $K_{M,n}^{\mathrm{fix}}$, equals the smallest number of ordered closest pairs. We show that reoptimizing the codebook at every SNR can yield a smaller leading coefficient $K_{M,n}^\ast$. Specifically, we show that the SNR-wise minimum exact maximum-likelihood error probability is $P_e^\ast=B_γ^\ast(K_{M,n}^\ast+o(1))$, while the minimum exact error over packing-optimal codebooks is $P_e^{\mathrm{fix}}=B_γ^\ast(K_{M,n}^{\mathrm{fix}}+o(1))$, where $B_γ^\ast$ is a common factor. We prove that $K_{M,n}^\ast\leq K_{M,n}^{\mathrm{fix}}$. Strict inequality, $K_{M,n}^\ast < K_{M,n}^{\mathrm{fix}}$ therefore gives a smaller exact error at all sufficiently high SNRs, i.e., $P_e^\ast<P_e^{\mathrm{fix}}$. We also characterize $K_{M,n}^\ast$ as the limit of the minimum Gaussian soft-packing energy.
  An orthoplex-bound construction explains strict inequality: an SNR-dependent perturbation makes one class of limiting closest pairs slightly closer, without changing the optimal exponent or their unit contributions to the constructed family's leading coefficient, while moving the remaining closest pairs farther apart on a larger but still vanishing scale, causing their contributions to vanish.
  For $2\leq M\leq n+1$, we prove $K_{M,n}^\ast=K_{M,n}^{\mathrm{fix}}=M(M-1)$. For $M=n+k$, $2\leq k\leq n$, we prove $K_{n+k,n}^{\mathrm{fix}}=4n(k-1)$ and $K_{n+k,n}^\ast\leq 4k(k-1)$. In particular, $K_{n+2,n}^\ast=8<4n=K_{n+2,n}^{\mathrm{fix}}$ for $n\geq3$, so SNR-wise optimization improves the asymptotic error by the factor $n/2$. At $k=n$, both coefficients equal $4n(n-1)$. For $3\leq k\leq n-1$, we conjecture $K_{n+k,n}^\ast=4k(k-1)$.

</details>


### [221] [Exact Common Information and Exact Channel Synthesis for Correlated Gaussian Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26012)
*Lei Yu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文解决了Yu和Tan于2020年提出的两个猜想，给出相关高斯源精确公共信息与精确信道合成速率区域的严格刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作对ρ相关高斯源的精确公共信息以及精确信道合成中的共享随机性率—通信率可达区域提出了猜想，但缺乏严格证明；同时需要澄清精确生成准则与全变差近似准则之间的本质差异。

Method: 作者结合最坏情形高斯交叉熵的精确最优传输表示、Fathi的高斯传输不等式，以及由条件均值协方差结构导出的行列式不等式，完成 converse 与可达性/区域刻画证明。

Result: 证明ρ相关高斯源的精确公共信息为 1/2 log((1+ρ)/(1-ρ)) + ρ/(1+ρ)，并证明精确信道合成中共享随机性率和通信率的可容许区域正是猜想区域。

Conclusion: 结果表明当ρ>0时，精确公共信息严格大于Wyner公共信息；同时，ρ相关高斯对的精确信道合成所需速率严格高于全变差意义下的信道合成。

Abstract: In this paper, we resolve two conjectures posed by Yu and Tan in 2020 (in two separate papers published in the IEEE Trans. Inf. Theory). Specifically, we establish that: 1) the exact common information for a pair of $ρ$-correlated Gaussian sources is given by the conjectured expression $\frac{1}{2}\log\frac{1+ρ}{1-ρ}+\fracρ{1+ρ}$; and 2) the admissible region for the shared randomness rate and the communication rate in exact channel synthesis is exactly the conjectured one. These results yield two important consequences. First, for any $ρ>0$, the exact common information of a correlated Gaussian pair strictly exceeds Wyner's common information. Second, for $ρ>0$, the exact channel synthesis of such a pair requires strictly higher rates than the total-variation version. The proof combines an exact optimal-transport representation of the worst-case Gaussian cross-entropy, Fathi's Gaussian transport inequality, and a determinant inequality arising from the covariance structure of the conditional means.

</details>
